.
We recently upgraded our fork mounts for the @rockymounts DriveShaft HM. We see them more and more in van builds, so we decided to give them a try. No need to slide-in the thru-axle, which make loading/unloading the bikes even easier, nice! They also have a key lock mechanism; one could simply unscrew the fork mount and leave with the bike, but bike thieves are dumb so they won't figure that out 🥴. We're happy with the upgrade and we recommend it! (For new builds we mean. You probably don't have to upgrade your actual build).
.
We also took the opportunity to upgrade our SLIDE-OUT BIKE RACK build article:
- We added a bunch of new photos;
- We made the steps more comprehensive;
- We added dimensions and stuff...
- ...Hope that helps!
.
-----------------------------------------------------------
🖱️: faroutride.com/slide-out-bike-rack (link in profile)
-----------------------------------------------------------
.
.
Cheers!
.
.
.
